🔔‌miniOrange 2FA V5.6.0 | What's New?🔔

[miniOrange 2FA]

miniOrange 2FA Plugin List of Latest Features

Setup Wizard

- Helps you to configure 2FA settings for your users right after activation
- Sets up 2FA method

Grace Period

- Allows you to set grace period for users to configure 2FA
- Locks out users if their 2FA is not set within grace period interval
- Admins can unblock users which are locked out

User Login Report

- Gives you login report of all the users
- Report available in WordPress submenu even when network security is disabled

Users' 2FA status

- Separate page to check 2FA status of users
- Opearations available : Reset 2FA, Unblock user

More Feature details

Website

miniOrange provides easy to use 2 Factor authentication for secure login to your WordPress site
...Know More

Documentation

miniOrange Two-Factor Authentication in which you have to provide two factors to gain the access

...Know More

Support

You are not going to hit a ridiculously long phone menu when you call us or contact us.

...Know More

Security site

miniOrange combines Web Application Firewall (WAF), Malware Scanner, Encrypted Database and File backup
...Know More

You are welcome to use our New Features

If you need any help we are just a mail away

Contact us at :- info@xecurify.com / 2fasupport@xecurify.com

🔔‌miniOrange 2FA V5.6.4 | What's New?🔔

[miniOrange 2FA]

miniOrange 2FA Plugin List of Latest Features

Setup Wizard

- Helps you to configure 2FA settings for your users right after activation
- Sets up 2FA method

Grace Period

- Allows you to set grace period for users to configure 2FA
- Enforce users to setup 2FA if their 2FA is not set within grace period interval

User Login Report

- Gives you login report of all the users
- Report available in WordPress submenu even when network security is disabled

Users' 2FA status

- Separate page to check 2FA status of users
- Opearations available : Reset 2FA, Unblock user

More Feature details

Website

miniOrange provides easy to use 2 Factor authentication for secure login to your WordPress site
...Know More

Documentation

miniOrange Two-Factor Authentication in which you have to provide two factors to gain the access

...Know More

Support

You are not going to hit a ridiculously long phone menu when you call us or contact us.

...Know More

Security site

miniOrange combines Web Application Firewall (WAF), Malware Scanner, Encrypted Database and File backup
...Know More

You are welcome to use our New Features

If you need any help we are just a mail away

Contact us at :- info@xecurify.com / 2fasupport@xecurify.com
